Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in parsing theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 633 and the invariant I(A,B) = 923 mod 1.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 633 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the parsing structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 923. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
